## Goal

Record the literal case-level ownership decisions, accepted catalog topology,
support-file destinations, implementation deferrals, and pilot path contracts
required by the suite-wide test audit.

This reference complements
[Test Suite Audit Inventory](test-suite-audit-inventory.md). The inventory
classifies every test and support file; this document resolves every suite
marked `case audit` or `split review`. The Phase 2 documentation and
test-quality reviews accepted each move or split as an implementation
boundary. `keep pending evidence` remains outside implementation scope.

In this reference, an accepted future target means the audit decision is
settled; it does not claim that the filesystem path already exists. A target
becomes current only after its implementation phase passes validation and adds
the corresponding path-correspondence row.

## Matrix Contract

Each scoped source declaration appears exactly once. A parameterized
declaration receives one row that names its literal title and variants rather
than one row per runtime expansion.

| Field            | Meaning                                                                  |
| ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Current test     | Exact nested source title or literal parameterized title                 |
| Claimed contract | Observable regression signal protected by the declaration                |
| Closest overlap  | Exact related path and title, or an em dash when none was identified     |
| Unique boundary  | Distinct seam, or the reason no distinct seam remains                    |
| Decision         | `move`, `split`, `rename`, `merge`, `remove`, or `keep pending evidence` |
| Target owner     | Exact future owner; merge and removal rows name the retained owner       |

## Coverage Summary

| Family                                                          | Inventory suites | Source declarations |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------: | ------------------: |
| Data Query and Data Query Codex                                 |               31 |                 184 |
| Markdown PDF                                                    |               32 |                 482 |
| Data Extract and Data Stack                                     |               14 |                 112 |
| Rename, Fonts, Video, Markdown Frontmatter, and release tooling |               10 |                  70 |
| CLI foundations and Doctor                                      |                7 |                 134 |
| **Total**                                                       |           **94** |             **982** |

Accepted decisions total 739 `move`, 215 `split`, 4 `rename`, 4 `merge`, 10
`remove`, and 10 `keep pending evidence` rows. The counts are implementation
boundaries, not a test-reduction target.

## Phase 2 Catalog And Support Decisions

### Accepted Catalog Shape

Use stable feature or platform owners, with one boundary directory only when it
groups multiple suites or support files:

```text
test/
  data-query/{direct,actions,commands,interactive,evidence}/
  doctor/{actions,commands,workflow,interactive,support}/
  data-sources/{adapters,fixtures}/
  cli-foundations/{dependencies,inline-rendering,interactive-harness}/
  markdown-pdf/{actions,adapters,interactive,support}/
  markdown-docx/actions/
  video/actions/
  <other-feature>/{direct,actions,commands,interactive,support}/
  helpers/ # independently reused, feature-neutral infrastructure only
```

`support` is not a default layer. Use it only for several feature-wide support
files; otherwise keep support beside its direct consumer boundary, such as
`data-query/actions/codex-support.ts`. No support row below qualifies for a
source-aligned exception: each has a clearer feature, platform, or shared
infrastructure owner. Any source-aligned exception admitted from the suite
inventory should remain separately justified by the four research criteria.

### Accepted Global Helper Ownership

Only these accepted future targets remain under global `test/helpers`:

| Target                                  | Independent consumer-family evidence                                                                                          | Feature-neutral semantic evidence                                                                                                                                                                                      | Required cleanup before admission                                                                             |
| --------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `test/helpers/cli-action-test-utils.ts` | 85 test consumers span Doctor, Data Stack, Markdown PDF, Data Query, Rename, Video, and other action families.                | Captured CLI runtime streams, no-output assertions, `CliError` assertions, and optional-path cleanup are generic action-test contracts; their production dependency is the feature-neutral CLI error/runtime platform. | Remove the Rename plan re-exports; Rename suites import their feature-owned support directly.                 |
| `test/helpers/cli-test-utils.ts`        | 125 test consumers span Data Query, Doctor, Markdown PDF, Fonts, release/version tooling, Rename, and other command families. | Built-CLI process launch, repository-relative paths, temporary fixture lifecycle, and captured streams are generic CLI test infrastructure; the only production type is the feature-neutral CLI runtime.               | Remove Rename-plan snapshot and cleanup from `runCli`; Rename owners perform their own feature-local cleanup. |
| `test/helpers/ansi.ts`                  | Data Preview action suites and Data Stack Interactive suites independently need ANSI removal.                                 | `stripAnsi` is a pure string transform, has no production-module dependency, uses no feature vocabulary, and has a stable assertion contract.                                                                          | Extract the current implementation from Data Preview support and update both feature families.                |

Import count alone is not the admission reason: the named families establish
independent use, and the post-split semantics establish neutrality. The
Interactive harness is not admitted as a global helper because its composition
and scenario schema intentionally know feature modules. It becomes a stable
`cli-foundations` platform owner, while feature mocks and feature scenario
contracts move to their owners.

### Phase 3 Data Query Path Contract

The mappings are suite-family contracts; literal case matrices still decide
case retention, rename, merge, or removal before the move.

## Phase 2 Data Query literal case matrices

### Summary

- Inventory suites: **31**.
- Declared `test` / `it` cases: **184**. Each `test.each` declaration is counted once at its literal declared title.
- Decisions: **keep pending evidence 10**, **move 171**, **split 3**.
- Removal rule: no case is merged or removed. Suspected command/action duplication without an exclusive literal seam remains `keep pending evidence`.
- Mixed-owner timeout suite: three single-feature routing declarations move to their exact owner; the three parameterized validation and help declarations split into Data Query and Data Stack command owners.

### Accepted Data Query Catalog

```text
test/
  data-query/
    action/
    command/
    interactive/
    codex-intent.test.ts
    header-mapping.test.ts
    source-introspection.test.ts
  data-stack/
    command/
      codex-timeout.test.ts
```

The three boundary folders are justified by multiple sibling suites and stable action, command, and Interactive seams. Pure feature helpers remain at the Data Query root. The Data Stack target is created only by splitting the mixed timeout registration suite.

## Phase 2 CLI Foundations And Doctor Case Matrices

Scope: the seven `case audit` or `split review` inventory rows owned by CLI
foundations, CLI Interactive infrastructure, Doctor, or the deferred
Doctor/DOCX/Video mix. The focused baseline passed **179 cases across 7 files**
with **1,107 assertions**. The matrices below cover each of the **134 source
`test` declarations exactly once**; parameterized declarations name their
literal source title and all runtime variants.

Decision vocabulary follows the audit contract. A `remove` or partial `split`
names the exact surviving owner. A move changes catalog ownership only and does
not authorize production behavior changes.

Runtime variants covered by the three fixture-loop declarations are:
`pandoc-missing`, `weasyprint-old`, `pandoc-unverified`, `sqlite-installable`,
`excel-constrained`, `sqlite-unknown`, `codex-unconfigured`,
`font-discovery-missing`, `font-coverage-missing-windows`,
`multiple-actions evidence`, `duckdb-and-codex-unconfigured`, and
`pandoc-missing-and-weasyprint-old`.

Count: 14 declarations, 52 runtime cases. Accepted audit result: 13 declarations
move intact; one parameterized declaration is split from four variants to one,
removing three runtime cases with explicit same-file retained owners.

Count: 35 declarations and 35 runtime cases. All cases move intact into ten
feature/platform destinations. No duplicate removal is justified: visually
similar action cases do not own command registration, option parsing,
forwarding, or the child-process exit/output boundary.

### Decision Totals

| Suite                      | Declarations | Runtime cases | Retained move/rename declarations | Merge/split declarations | Removed declarations | Removed runtime cases |
| -------------------------- | -----------: | ------------: | --------------------------------: | -----------------------: | -------------------: | --------------------: |
| `cli-action-doctor`        |           14 |            52 |                                13 |                        1 |                    0 |            3 variants |
| deferred Doctor/DOCX/Video |           29 |            36 |                                24 |                        0 |                    5 |                     5 |
| contextual tip             |           12 |            12 |                                 8 |                        4 |                    0 |                     0 |
| Interactive menu           |            7 |             7 |                                 4 |                        0 |                    3 |                     3 |
| top-level routing          |           14 |            14 |                                12 |                        0 |                    2 |                     2 |
| inline text                |           23 |            23 |                                23 |                        0 |                    0 |                     0 |
| CLI UX                     |           35 |            35 |                                35 |                        0 |                    0 |                     0 |
| **Total**                  |      **134** |       **179** |                           **119** |                    **5** |               **10** |                **13** |

The accepted post-audit set is 120 source declarations and 166 runtime cases.
The 13-case reduction is evidence-based: 10 whole removals and three duplicated
variants removed from one parameterized Doctor declaration. The two
contextual-tip owners become table-driven across all three flows, preserving
flow-to-catalog dispatch while consolidating four source declarations. The
complete action-level Markdown PDF readiness projection remains alongside the
pure requirements matrix because they protect different layers.

`cli-foundations` owns only reusable terminal/menu/command infrastructure.
Data, Doctor, Markdown, Rename, and Video behavior leaves the platform bucket
even when it was discovered in a root smoke suite. Doctor fixtures remain
feature-local because their vocabulary and expected reports are Doctor-specific.

### Exact Phase 4 Doctor Slice

#### Pre-change owners

```text
test/cli-action-doctor.test.ts
test/cli-actions-doctor-markdown-video-deferred.test.ts
test/cli-command-doctor.test.ts
test/cli-doctor-workflow.test.ts
test/cli-interactive-menu.test.ts
test/cli-interactive-routing.test.ts
test/helpers/doctor-test-fixtures.ts
test/helpers/interactive-harness/mocks/action-misc.ts
```

The mixed deferred suite must be split in the same phase so no residual
Doctor/DOCX/Video bucket survives. One DuckDB declaration is removed in favor
of the Data Query command owner; the direct invalid-input declaration moves to
the Data Query action owner. Dependency-parser declarations move to CLI
foundations, while DOCX and Video declarations move to their feature owners.
Only Doctor cases move out of the two root Interactive suites; their non-Doctor
residues remain at the root for Phase 6.

The decision totals above cover the eventual CLI and feature catalog. Phase 4
applies the 11 evidence-approved runtime removals from the core action, mixed,
and Doctor menu owners. The two accepted non-Doctor top-level routing removals
remain unchanged at the root until Phase 6.

moves the distinct direct invalid-input contract to
`test/data-query/actions/duckdb-lifecycle.test.ts`; it does not move or rewrite
the retained command suite. The Doctor mock moves to its feature owner while
the non-Doctor support residue stays at its current path. The post-change
Interactive targets exercise both sides of that support split; the support
modules are not standalone Bun test targets.

### Decision Summary

- Inventory suites: 14.
- Declared `test`/`it` cases: 112.
- Literal matrix rows: 112.
- Decisions: 111 `move`, 1 `split`, 0 `keep`, 0 `rename`, 0 `merge`, 0 `remove`, and 0 `keep pending evidence`.
- No merge or removal is accepted. The overlaps consistently stop at a distinct action, built-command, Interactive, replay, artifact-I/O, or direct-reporting seam.
- The one split moves the direct `renderDataStackUnionByNameAliasWarning` case out of the built-command options suite; its retained owner is `test/data-stack/direct/reporting.test.ts`.

The neighboring retained Data Extract action suites, Data Stack Interactive suites, and Data Stack direct/unit suites are outside this 14-row matrix. Their eventual move should use the same owner folders without changing the case decisions recorded here.

### Deferrals And Exceptions

- No source-aligned exception is accepted for these 14 suites.
- Their physical moves are temporarily deferred to the remaining-feature batch. Revisit when the Data Query and Doctor pilots have both accepted the feature/boundary catalog pattern and Phase 6 begins.
- Data Extract action/command fixture ownership remains subject to the separate support-file ownership review. That support decision must not collapse the action and command test seams retained here.
- Data Stack Interactive support still re-exports Data Preview `stripAnsi`; resolve that support leak separately without moving Interactive cases into Data Preview.
- The direct warning-rendering split is an ownership correction, not evidence that the built-command warning case is redundant: TTY ANSI scope and actual command warning emission remain different contracts.

bun test test/cli-actions-data-stack/*.test.ts test/cli-command-data-stack/*.test.ts test/cli-interactive-data-stack/*.test.ts test/cli-interactive-routing-data-stack.test.ts

### Validation Evidence

- Inventory filtering reproduced exactly 14 scoped paths, and a sorted diff against the scope manifest was empty.
- Static declaration extraction reproduced exactly 112 nested `describe > test/it` titles; a sorted diff against the 112 matrix titles was empty and all matrix titles were unique.
- Every matrix row has six data cells plus leading/trailing table delimiters, an allowed decision, and an exact accepted future `test/` target.
- Decision recount reproduced 111 `move` rows and 1 `split` row.
- Data Extract focused command: 55 passed, 0 failed, 264 assertions across 13 files.
- Data Stack focused command: 122 passed, 0 failed, 639 assertions across 12 files.

## Phase 2 Markdown PDF Case Matrices

This section covers every declared `test` or `it` case in the 32 inventory
rows whose feature owner begins `Markdown PDF` and whose disposition is
`case audit` or `split review`. A case appears once by source declaration,
including one row for a parameterized declaration rather than one row per
runtime expansion.

Safety, privacy, cleanup, collision, renderer, and reproducible-evidence
contracts remain preserved. An exact duplicate title is treated only as an
overlap signal, not proof of equivalent triggers, layers, outcomes, side
effects, or cleanup.

### Validation Summary

| Check                                                  | Result                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Inventory suites selected                              | 32                                                                                                           |
| Literal case declarations covered                      | 482                                                                                                          |
| `move` decisions                                       | 278                                                                                                          |
| `split` decisions                                      | 204                                                                                                          |
| `keep`, `rename`, `merge`, `remove` decisions          | 0 each                                                                                                       |
| `keep pending evidence` decisions                      | 0                                                                                                            |
| Exact duplicate declared leaf titles across this slice | 1 pair; retained in distinct Profile and Template action owners                                              |
| Merge or removal candidates                            | None; no literal equivalence was proved                                                                      |
| Rename-only candidates                                 | None; parameter placeholders such as `%s`, `$label`, and `${action}` are exact declared parameterized titles |

The matrix admits movement and bounded splitting only. It does not authorize
production changes or test deletion.

### Mixed Markdown PDF Pilot Recommendation

Use the Bundle plus Interactive font-hints pair as the bounded Phase 5 pilot.
The pair is intentionally mixed: Bundle covers action-level discovery,
resolution, conflict safety, and render integration, while font hints covers
Interactive modeling, asynchronous discovery, cancellation, keyboard behavior,
and post-Codex review. Both already have literal boundaries that support small
second-level folders without moving the entire Markdown PDF family.

Pre-change focused command:

```bash
bun test test/cli-actions-md-to-pdf-bundle.test.ts test/cli-interactive-markdown-pdf/font-hints.test.ts
```

Expected post-change focused command:

```bash
bun test test/markdown-pdf/actions/bundle-discovery.test.ts test/markdown-pdf/actions/bundle-resolution.test.ts test/markdown-pdf/actions/bundle-integration.test.ts test/markdown-pdf/interactive/font-model.test.ts test/markdown-pdf/interactive/font-suggestion-service.test.ts test/markdown-pdf/interactive/font-post-codex-review.test.ts
```

Adjacent safety check for the pilot:

```bash
bun test test/cli-actions-md-to-pdf-actions-assets.test.ts test/cli-actions-md-to-pdf-actions-validation.test.ts test/cli-interactive-markdown-pdf/lifecycle.test.ts test/markdown-pdf-page-number-renderer-evidence/orchestration.test.ts
```

### Mechanical Receipt

- The source manifest is the sorted 32-row inventory selection described above.
- AST extraction found 482 string or template-literal case declarations.
- Each declaration key `current path:source line` is represented once.
- Parameterized declarations remain one row each with their exact declared title.
- Every target begins with `test/markdown-pdf/`.
- Every decision is one of the admitted case-matrix values.
- No merge or removal is accepted, so no retained-owner receipt is required.

## Phase 2 Miscellaneous Feature Case Matrices

### Audit Boundary

This section covers exactly the ten inventory rows owned by `Rename`,
`Fonts`, `Release tooling`, `Video`, or `Markdown Frontmatter` whose Phase 1
disposition is `case audit` or `split review`:

1. `test/adapters-codex-rename-timeout.test.ts`
2. `test/cli-actions-md-frontmatter-to-json.test.ts`
3. `test/cli-actions-rename-codex-internals.test.ts`

8. `test/fonts-cli-check-provider.test.ts`
9. `test/fonts-cli-inspect-matching.test.ts`
10. `test/release-scripts.test.ts`

Every declared `test(...)` case appears once below. The empty
`describe("font coverage", () => {})` declaration is recorded separately
because it contains no test case. “Closest overlap” names an exact existing
path and nested title when one exists; an em dash means the repository-wide
title and symbol scan found no close test owner.

| `Codex rename adapter timeout summaries > image adapter preserves the generic summary for unknown failures`               | An image batch result carrying an unknown adapter error is wrapped in the stable generic title-generation summary.                                                | `test/adapters-codex-shared.test.ts` > `codex shared adapter helpers > keeps an exhausted unknown failure on the unchanged generic summary path`     | The shared case owns generic exhaustion metadata; this case owns the image adapter's wrapping of a returned `errorMessage`.                                  | rename   | `test/rename/adapters/image-title-suggester.test.ts`; rename to `wraps returned image adapter errors in the generic title-generation summary`. |
| `Codex rename adapter timeout summaries > image production batch builds the SDK request and parses its response`          | Production image-title execution sends structured text plus local-image input, schema, working directory, and timeout signal, then normalizes the response title. | —                                                                                                                                                    | This is the only direct image production-thread request-shape and response-translation test.                                                                 | move     | `test/rename/adapters/image-title-suggester.test.ts`; retain title.                                                                            |
| `Codex rename adapter timeout summaries > image production retries create a fresh timeout signal for each attempt`        | Each production retry creates and forwards a new timeout signal while preserving a later successful suggestion.                                                   | `test/adapters-codex-shared.test.ts` > `codex shared adapter helpers > executeBatchesWithRetries retries thrown errors and succeeds`                 | The shared case owns retry control flow; this case owns per-attempt `AbortSignal.timeout` creation at the production SDK seam.                               | move     | `test/rename/adapters/image-title-suggester.test.ts`; retain title.                                                                            |
| `Codex rename adapter timeout summaries > document adapter uses the shared default and recognizes a wrapped timeout`      | Document-title batching defaults to 30 seconds and translates a timeout preserved through an error cause into the document-specific summary.                      | `test/adapters-codex-failure.test.ts` > `Codex request failure classification > recognizes a TimeoutError through a preserved cause chain`           | Failure classification is tested directly elsewhere; this case owns document-adapter defaulting and its externally visible summary.                          | move     | `test/rename/adapters/document-title-suggester.test.ts`; retain title.                                                                         |
| `Codex rename adapter timeout summaries > document production batch builds the SDK request and classifies its timeout`    | Production document-title execution extracts document text, sends schema and signal to the SDK, and reports the configured wrapped timeout.                       | `test/adapters-codex-document-rename-titles.test.ts` > `codex document rename title extractor > extracts docx evidence from a metadata-rich fixture` | The extractor suite owns evidence extraction; this case uniquely couples extracted text to production SDK request wiring and timeout translation.            | move     | `test/rename/adapters/document-title-suggester.test.ts`; retain title.                                                                         |

Suite result: split by image and document adapter ownership. The generic batch
retry tests remain in the shared Codex adapter suite; none of these six cases
has the same trigger, outcome, failure signal, layer, and side-effect lifecycle
as its closest overlap.

| `cli action modules: md frontmatter-to-json > parses TOML frontmatter and reports frontmatterType=toml`                         | TOML delimiters and values are parsed and identified as TOML in the action result.                                               | —                                                                                                  | No other case exercises TOML through this action.                                                                  | move     | `test/markdown-frontmatter/actions/frontmatter-to-json.test.ts`; retain title.            |

| `cli action modules: md frontmatter-to-json > rejects missing frontmatter`                                                      | The action raises `FRONTMATTER_NOT_FOUND` with exit 2 and no output.                                                             | Same file > `cli command: md frontmatter-to-json > returns an error for missing frontmatter`       | The action case owns the typed error and no-output invariant before process rendering.                             | move     | `test/markdown-frontmatter/actions/frontmatter-to-json.test.ts`; retain title.            |

| `cli command: md frontmatter-to-json > prints wrapper JSON by default`                                                          | The built CLI registers the route, accepts `-i`, forwards the default mode, exits zero, and renders wrapper JSON only to stdout. | Same file > `cli action modules: md frontmatter-to-json > emits wrapper JSON to stdout by default` | The unique boundary is command registration, short-option parsing, forwarding, and process streams.                | move     | `test/markdown-frontmatter/commands/frontmatter-to-json.test.ts`; retain title.           |
| `cli command: md frontmatter-to-json > supports --data-only and --pretty flags`                                                 | The built CLI parses and jointly forwards `--data-only` and `--pretty`, producing pretty data-only JSON.                         | Same file > `cli action modules: md frontmatter-to-json > supports data-only and pretty output`    | The unique boundary is parser and boolean-option forwarding rather than action serialization.                      | move     | `test/markdown-frontmatter/commands/frontmatter-to-json.test.ts`; retain title.           |
| `cli command: md frontmatter-to-json > returns an error for missing frontmatter`                                                | The built CLI maps the missing-frontmatter action error to exit 2, empty stdout, and user-facing stderr.                         | Same file > `cli action modules: md frontmatter-to-json > rejects missing frontmatter`             | The unique boundary is process-level error rendering and exit behavior.                                            | move     | `test/markdown-frontmatter/commands/frontmatter-to-json.test.ts`; retain title.           |

Suite result: split action and command layers. The apparent action/command
overlap is intentional because each command case asserts parser, forwarding,
or process behavior that the corresponding direct action case cannot protect.

| Current test exact nested title                                                                                    | Claimed contract                                                                                                                                         | Closest overlap exact path and title                                                                                           | Unique boundary                                                                 | Decision | Target owner                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| `release workflow helpers > filters allowed release branches by remote-prefix policy`                              | The shell filter admits exact and prefixed alpha, beta, canary, and dev remote branches while rejecting incidental substrings and nested lookalikes.     | —                                                                                                                              | This is the only executable release-branch policy test.                         | move     | `test/release-tooling/branch-filter.test.ts`; retain title. |
| `stable release notes script > uses CHANGELOGS override content when a matching stable release note exists`        | A matching tagged stable-note path replaces generated content and preserves its full-changelog link.                                                     | —                                                                                                                              | This is the canonical manual-override precedence case.                          | move     | `test/release-tooling/stable-notes.test.ts`; retain title.  |
| `stable release notes script > reads CHANGELOGS override content from the tagged tree instead of branch head`      | Stable publication reads the release note from the tagged tree even when branch head later rewrites the same path.                                       | Same file > `stable release notes script > uses CHANGELOGS override content when a matching stable release note exists`        | Override presence and immutable tagged-source selection are separate contracts. | move     | `test/release-tooling/stable-notes.test.ts`; retain title.  |
| `stable release notes script > fails when a matching CHANGELOGS override file is empty`                            | A present but whitespace-only tagged override is a hard error with a path-specific message.                                                              | Same file > `stable release notes script > falls back to generated notes when no matching CHANGELOGS override file exists`     | Empty and absent overrides intentionally diverge, so both cases are required.   | move     | `test/release-tooling/stable-notes.test.ts`; retain title.  |
| `stable release notes script > falls back to generated notes when no matching CHANGELOGS override file exists`     | Missing matching override generates categorized notes and comparison link while ignoring unrelated note files.                                           | Same file > `stable release notes script > fails when a matching CHANGELOGS override file is empty`                            | This owns the allowed absence path and generated fallback body.                 | move     | `test/release-tooling/stable-notes.test.ts`; retain title.  |
| `stable release notes script > hybrid mode deduplicates grouped entries for multi-commit pull requests`            | Hybrid generation groups two commits for one PR into one release entry and one changelog entry.                                                          | Same file > `stable release notes script > groups PR-backed entries using the resolved PR title instead of the commit subject` | This owns PR-number de-duplication independent of title resolution.             | move     | `test/release-tooling/stable-notes.test.ts`; retain title.  |
| `stable release notes script > groups PR-backed entries using the resolved PR title instead of the commit subject` | When API metadata resolves a PR, grouping and attribution use its title, category, author, and repository comparison URL rather than the commit subject. | Same file > `stable release notes script > hybrid mode deduplicates grouped entries for multi-commit pull requests`            | This owns API-resolved metadata precedence and attribution, not de-duplication. | move     | `test/release-tooling/stable-notes.test.ts`; retain title.  |

Suite result: split the branch-filter script from stable-note generation. The
stable-note cases form one coherent integration owner and should not be split
further merely because each creates a separate fixture repository. Move the
shared command runner and temporary repository setup to the exact feature-local
support target `test/release-tooling/fixtures.ts`.

### Decision Summary

- Audited suites: 10 of 10 assigned inventory rows.
- Declared `test(...)` cases: 70 of 70 represented exactly once.
- Case decisions: 63 `move`, 6 `split`, 1 `rename`, 0 `merge`, 0 `remove`,
  and 0 `keep pending evidence`.
- Non-case structure: remove one empty `describe("font coverage", () => {})`.
- Accepted stable owners: `rename`, `markdown-frontmatter`, `video`, `fonts`,
  and platform owner `release-tooling`.
- Accepted future target files: 21 exact catalog paths across those five owners.
- Accepted source-aligned exceptions: none. Each assigned suite has a stable
  feature or platform owner, and the split families have enough suites or a
  stable production seam to justify their boundary folders.
- Accepted temporary root deferrals: none. No assigned suite is a lone,
  uncertain owner that needs to remain at `test/` while another decision
  lands.
- Proven duplicate test removals: none. Similar cases differ in layer,
  trigger, output form, failure signal, or side-effect lifecycle.

The six `split` decisions expand to more than six future cases; their current
single declarations contain multiple independently failing contracts. Phase 2
accepted these destinations. Implementation remains gated by the focused
validation, correspondence update, and exact-range review required by the
destination phase.
